### [1] [Light-cone distributions of vector and scalar bound states in the Schwinger model from tensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31745)
*Juan Hormaza,Wenyang Qian*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用张量网络方法，在具有质量的Schwinger模型中直接在Minkowski时空计算了矢量与标量介子的 parton 分布函数（PDFs）和轻锥分布振幅（LCDAs），克服了欧氏格点方法无法直接处理实时间关联的限制；结果揭示了电荷共轭对轻锥结构的决定性影响，并验证了不同方法提取衰变常数的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Light-cone 分布（如 PDFs 和 LCDAs）表征强子的组分结构，但其依赖实时间关联，而标准欧氏格点QCD方法仅适用于虚时间，因此亟需发展能在 Minkowski 时空直接计算 light-cone 关联函数的新方法。

Method: 采用 Kogut-Susskind 格点哈密顿量形式，通过 Gauss 定律消去规范场自由度；将真空与介子束缚态表示为矩阵乘积态（MPS）；利用实时间演化直接计算规范不变的 light-cone 关联函数；依据电荷共轭 C 和动量量子数识别矢量（C = −1）与标量（C = +1）介子。

Result: 矢量介子 PDF 单峰、LCDA 对称；标量介子 PDF 双峰、LCDA 反对称（零矩为零，需以一阶矩归一化）；由局域矩阵元与 LCDA 矩导出的衰变常数一致；大费米子质量下 PDF ≈ |LCDA|²，表明价夸克主导。

Conclusion: 张量网络方法可高效、精确地计算低维规范理论中基态及激发强子的 light-cone 分布，为未来在 (3+1)D QCD 中拓展提供了原理性验证与技术路径。

Abstract: Light-cone distributions encode the partonic structure of hadrons, but they involve real-time correlations that Euclidean lattice methods cannot access directly. We compute the parton distribution functions (PDFs) and light-cone distribution amplitudes (LCDAs) of the vector and scalar meson bound states of the massive Schwinger model with tensor networks. Using the Kogut-Susskind lattice Hamiltonian with the gauge field eliminated through Gauss's law, we prepare the vacuum and the mesons as matrix product states and identify the vector and scalar mesons through their charge conjugation and momentum. The gauge-invariant light-cone correlators that define both distributions are evaluated directly in Minkowski space by real-time evolution. The two mesons exhibit qualitatively different light-cone structure, dictated by charge conjugation. The vector meson has a single-peaked PDF and a symmetric LCDA, while the scalar meson has a double-peaked PDF and an antisymmetric LCDA, whose vanishing zeroth moment requires a normalization through its first moment. The decay constants obtained from local matrix elements and from the moments of the LCDAs agree, and approach predictions from nonrelativistic model as the fermion mass increases. For the relatively large masses considered in this work, the PDFs closely follow the squared LCDAs, showing that the mesons are dominated by their valence component. Our results demonstrate tensor networks as a practical route to light-cone distributions of ground-state as well as excited hadrons in low-dimensional gauge theories.

</details>


### [2] [Parton Physics in the Schwinger Model from Matrix Product States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31749)
*Mari Carmen Bañuls,Krzysztof Cichy,C.-J. David Lin,Manuel Schneider*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在Minkowski时空哈密顿框架下直接计算部分子分布函数（PDFs）和轻锥分布振幅（LCDAs）的新方法，利用张量网络（MPS）表征希尔伯特空间态，并通过分步实现光锥威尔逊线，首次在施温格模型中系统计算了费米子/反费米子PDF、LCDA及矢量介子衰变常数，量化了所有系统误差并验证了物理约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统欧几里得格点QCD难以处理含光锥方向威尔逊线的非微扰矩阵元（如PDF/LCDA），因其本质为Minkowski时空定义的光前算符；亟需发展可直接在光前或Minkowski框架下计算这些结构函数的原理性新方法。

Method: 采用哈密顿格点方法，在Minkowski时空下构建施温格模型的离散化光前哈密顿量；用矩阵乘积态（MPS）表示多体态， bond dimension 控制张量网络表达能力；光锥威尔逊线通过交替执行外部电荷的空间平移与Trotter化的含时演化实现；系统不确定性由格距、体积、bond dimension、规范场截断、Trotter步长等多参数外推量化。

Result: 首次在施温格模型中数值计算了全范围费米子质量下的费米子/反费米子PDF、矢量介子LCDA及衰变常数；验证了归一化条件、动量求和规则（∫x q(x)dx = 1/2）及电荷共轭对称性；证实纠缠熵增长缓慢，表明中等bond dimension已足够高精度——支持该方法向QCD推广的可行性。

Conclusion: 张量网络哈密顿方法为第一性原理计算光前分布函数提供了严格可行路径；本工作确立了Minkowski格点+MPS框架在非微扰强子结构研究中的有效性与可控性，是迈向现实QCD PDF/LCDA格点计算的关键原理验证。

Abstract: Parton distribution functions (PDFs) and light-cone distribution amplitudes (LCDAs) encode the non-perturbative structure of hadrons in terms of their partonic constituents. They are defined through matrix elements containing parton operators that are connected by a Wilson line along a light-front direction, which makes them difficult to access in Euclidean lattice calculations. We demonstrate how parton functions can be directly obtained in Minkowski space using the Hamiltonian framework. We compute fermion and antifermion PDFs, LCDAs, and decay constants of the vector meson in the Schwinger model for several fermion masses. States in Hilbert space are efficiently represented by tensor networks, with an expressivity that is systematically controlled by a bond dimension. The light-front Wilson line is implemented as a stepwise evolution on the lattice that alternates spatial shifts of an external charge with Trotterized time-evolution steps acting on a matrix product state. All systematic uncertainties are quantified by varying and extrapolating in the lattice spacing, volume, bond dimension, gauge-field truncation, and Trotter step width. The slow growth of the entanglement entropy makes moderate bond dimensions sufficient, which supports the feasibility of using tensor networks for computing light-front matrix elements in more general settings. We confirm that the computed distributions fulfill the expected normalizations, momentum sum rules, and symmetry properties.

</details>


### [3] [Prospects and limitations of adaptive variational quantum algorithms for lattice field theories: a case study in 2+1 dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33138)
*Nico Dichter,Andrea Bulgarelli,Lena Funcke,Stefan Kühn*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究ADAPT-VQE算法在高于1+1维格点规范理论中的适用性，指出其存在两个普适限制：实哈密顿量与实初态下实池算符梯度恒为零；算法易收敛至激发态。作者以2+1维自由Wilson费米子为例验证上述问题，并通过引入电荷共轭对称性及组合不同算符池与初态成功恢复基态与第一激发态，为高维格点场论的变分量子模拟提供实践指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展ADAPT-VQE在高维（>1+1D）格点规范/场论真空态制备中的应用，检验其鲁棒性与局限性。

Method: 理论分析ADAPT-VQE梯度消失与激发态收敛机制；数值模拟2+1D自由Wilson费米子模型；构造含电荷共轭对称性的扩展算符池；组合多池与对称性匹配初态以实现能级选择性制备。

Result: 证实ADAPT-VQE在实哈密顿量-实初态下因时间反演对称性导致实池算符梯度为零；观察到算法收敛至激发态；引入电荷共轭对称性池并联合初态可精确复现全质量区间的基态与第一激发态能量及结构。

Conclusion: ADAPT-VQE在高维格点场论中需谨慎设计算符池与初态以规避对称性导致的梯度消失和非目标能级收敛；对称性嵌入与池组合是可行且必要的策略。

Abstract: The variational quantum algorithm ADAPT-VQE has previously been used to prepare the vacuum of lattice gauge theories in $1+1D$. In this paper, we study whether it extends to higher dimensions. We identify two general limitations of ADAPT-VQE. First, if the qubit Hamiltonian and the initial state are real, i.e., invariant under qubit-level time-reversal, all real-valued pool operators have vanishing gradients and are never selected. Second, the algorithm can converge to excited eigenstates. We illustrate both limitations, also providing examples from literature, and present a first application of ADAPT-VQE to a lattice field theory in $2+1D$, namely free Wilson fermions, where both obstacles occur. Adding charge conjugation symmetry to the operator pool improves the results, and combining different pools with suitable initial states recovers the ground and first excited states over the full mass range. These findings give practical guidelines for applying ADAPT-VQE to lattice field theories beyond $1+1D$.

</details>


### [4] [Intrinsic Soft Function in the Continuum and Physical Mass Limit from Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31810)
*Zhi-Chao Gong,Min-Huan Chu,Jun Hua,Xiangdong Ji,Andreas Schäfer,Jin-Xin Tan,Han-Zhang Wang,Wei Wang,Jian-Hui Zhang,Mu-Hua Zhang,Qi-An Zhang*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次在格点QCD中同时实现连续极限、物理π介子质量与无穷动量极限下，利用大动量有效理论（LaMET）从轻介子形状因子和准TMD波函数中提取本征软函数；通过幂次计数引导的轴矢量插值算符显著提升信噪比，实现了物理π质量下的直接计算，并在b_⊥≈1.1 fm范围内获得高精度结果，其横动量空间行为呈现近似指数衰减，有效标度Λ≈0.7 GeV（μ=2 GeV），为非微扰Wilson线动力学提供定量基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本征软函数是横动量依赖（TMD）因子化中普适的非微扰成分，但此前尚无格点QCD计算能同时控制连续极限、物理π质量及无穷动量极限。

Method: 采用大动量有效理论（LaMET），结合轻介子电磁形状因子与准TMD波函数的格点计算；引入幂次计数指导的轴矢量插值算符以压制高阶幂次修正并增强信号；使用覆盖四个格距的六组格点系综进行外推。

Result: 首次获得同时满足连续极限、物理π质量与无穷动量极限的本征软函数，横距离b_⊥可达≈1.1 fm；其b_⊥依赖呈近似指数衰减，提取出有效非微扰标度Λ≈0.7 GeV（重整化尺度μ=2 GeV）；短距行为与微扰QCD预言一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为TMD因子化的非微扰输入提供了首个严格可控的格点基准，验证了LaMET框架下软函数提取的可行性，并确立了描述Wilson线横向结构的关键非微扰参数Λ。

Abstract: The intrinsic soft function is a universal nonperturbative ingredient of transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) factorization. Despite substantial progress in lattice QCD studies, a determination with simultaneous control of the continuum, physical-pion-mass, and infinite-momentum limits has remained unavailable. In this work we present the first lattice determination incorporating all these limits within large-momentum effective theory, extracting the intrinsic soft function from a light-meson form factor and quasi-TMD wave functions. A key advance is the use of power-counting-guided axial-vector interpolators, which substantially improve the signal and enable a direct calculation at the physical pion mass. Combined with results from six ensembles spanning four lattice spacings, this improvement allows a precise determination extending to $b_\perp\simeq1.1~\mathrm{fm}$, while remaining consistent with perturbative predictions at short distances. The resulting transverse-distance dependence exhibits an approximately exponential decay, with the decay rate being characterized by an effective scale $\Lambda\simeq0.7~\mathrm{GeV}$ at $\mu=2~\mathrm{GeV}$, providing a quantitative benchmark for nonperturbative Wilson-line dynamics and essential input for precision TMD studies.

</details>


### [5] [Bound-state preparation in strongly coupled theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33079)
*Scott Lawrence,Duff Neill*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类无需量子相位估计算法的复合态制备方法，利用弱耦合辅助场在强耦合理论（包括非阿贝尔规范理论）中制备渐近态（如束缚态），并通过一维非相对论理论的经典模拟验证，并估算其在QCD中J/ψ产生过程的应用效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合量子场论中难以通过绝热演化从自由理论出发制备渐近态（如束缚态），而此类态是散射模拟等关键量子模拟任务的基础输入。

Method: 引入弱耦合辅助场与目标强耦合系统相互作用，构造可控制演化的扩展哈密顿量，通过变分优化或受控动力学实现目标希尔伯特子空间中最低激发态（即所需渐近态）的高保真度制备；全过程规避量子相位估计。

Result: 在经典模拟的一维非相对论模型中验证了该算法的有效性与鲁棒性；对QCD中J/ψ产生的产率进行了初步量化估算，表明该方法具有实际应用潜力。

Conclusion: 该辅助场驱动的态制备框架为强耦合规范理论的量子模拟提供了可扩展、免相位估计的新范式，尤其适用于当前含噪声中等规模量子（NISQ）设备。

Abstract: Many quantum simulation tasks, including scattering in quantum field theories and effective non-relativistic theories, require the preparation of asymptotic states. Such a state, which may be a bound state, is typically the lowest-lying excited state in some sector of Hilbert space. At weak coupling these states can often be prepared adiabatically by beginning with the free theory, but this is not generally feasible in strongly coupled theories. We detail a family of algorithms for preparing composite states in strongly coupled theories, including non-Abelian gauge theories, by using weakly coupled auxiliary fields. These methods do not require quantum phase estimation. We demonstrate the method numerically, via classical simulation, on nonrelativistic theories in one spatial dimension. We estimate the yield of this method applied to $J/\psi$ production in quantum chromodynamics.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在数值相对论模拟中直接到达未来零无穷（ℐ⁺），需发展稳定、可靠的三维双曲面演化方法。

Method: 采用共形紧化框架，将BSSN与Z4形式从球对称推广至全三维，并结合双曲面切片条件与适当边界处理，在无反射边界下实现自由演化。

Result: 成功实现了三维非线性双曲面演化；对Minkowski背景上的规范波与约束违反引力波进行数值模拟，证实约束守恒良好、波形在ℐ⁺处可稳定提取。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了首个基于通用广义相对论公式的三维自由双曲面数值演化方案，为后续黑洞并合等强场辐射问题的ℐ⁺波形建模奠定基础。

Abstract: Hyperboloidal evolution allows to reach future null infinity within numerical relativity simulations. This work extends the conformal compactification approach with BSSN/Z4 from spherical symmetry to full 3D simulations. This is the first free numerical hyperboloidal evolution in 3D with a common formulation of general relativity. In this first step, gauge waves and constraint-violating gravitational waves perturbing Minkowski spacetime are evolved nonlinearly and show that the implementation is reliable and signals can be successfully extracted at future null infinity.

</details>


### [7] [How to Play a Black Hole: Understanding Quasinormal Mode Excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31381)
*Lucía Vélez-Tartajo,Vitor Cardoso,Jaime Redondo-Yuste*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了施瓦西黑洞准正规模（QNM）在受控探针下的激发机制，揭示了初始数据局域性、源运动轨迹及强场区域贡献对QNM激发与环down信号构成的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞准正规模的激发机制对于引力波信号解释及黑洞参数反演至关重要。

Method: 采用数值相对论与频域/时域扰动分析方法，系统考察不同初始数据分布（宽泛vs. 光环局域）、下落粒子源（plunging source）动力学，以及人为截断源项（finite-radius cutoff）对QNM激发谱和晚期尾项（tail）相对强度的影响。

Result: 1) 宽泛初始数据抑制QNM激发、增强晚期幂律尾；2) 局域于光球附近的初始数据显著压制高频欠谐波（overtones），凸显基模；3) 下落粒子产生双阶段ringdown，分别对应于下落散射辐射与穿越光球时刻的激发；4) 源在有限半径处关闭所生成波形，随截断半径指数趋近标准下落波形，表明ringdown主要源于强场区（r ≲ 3M）。

Conclusion: QNM激发具有强动力学与几何选择性：光球附近是QNM主导激发区，而欠谐波激发更敏感于初始数据的空间局域性；ringdown信号本质上是强场时空响应的指纹，其组成可由源的时空轨迹与支撑集精确调控。

Abstract: Understanding the excitation of black hole quasinormal modes is essential for interpreting gravitational-wave signals and inferring black hole properties from them. In this work, we investigate the excitation of the quasinormal modes of Schwarzschild black holes under controlled probes. In the absence of a source, we find that broader initial data suppresses mode excitation, enhancing the relative contribution of the late-time tail, while data localized near the light ring strongly suppresses overtones relative to the fundamental mode. On the other hand, plunging sources give rise to two distinct ringdown stages, associated with scattered radiation generated during the particle's infall and with the particle crossing the light ring. Finally, we investigate how much of the signal is sourced from the strong-field region by switching off the source at some finite radius. The resulting waveform is exponentially close -- in cutoff radius -- to that of a standard infall. These results provide insight into the dependence of QNM excitation on the properties and dynamics of the perturbation, and offer a controlled setting in which to investigate the origin of black hole ringdown.

</details>

*Anna Liu,Mick Wright,Jef Heynen,Eungwang Seo,Isaac C. F. Wong,Disha Hegde,Laura E. Uronen,Harsh Narola,Chris Van Den Broeck,Martin Hendry,Tjonnie G. F. Li,Otto A. Hannuksela,Justin Janquart*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了引力波事件GW231123是否可能由引力透镜效应导致，发现透镜解释在四种波形模型下均具统计支持（3.96σ），并缓解了不同模型间对自旋与质量推断的分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW231123是迄今观测到质量最大的双黑洞并合事件，其高推断质量与自旋参数对波形模型高度敏感，而引力透镜尚未被实证观测；因此需检验透镜是否可解释该异常特征。

Method: 将信号建模为同一并合事件经引力透镜产生的多个重叠像的叠加，并在四种独立波形模型下进行贝叶斯模型比较与参数估计，同时与无透镜背景样本对比以评估显著性。

Result: 透镜模型在所有四类波形中均获支持；推断自旋显著降低，质量与自旋在不同模型间一致性增强；透镜解释的统计显著性为3.96^{+0.68}_{-0.30}σ。

Conclusion: 引力透镜是解释GW231123异常参数的有力候选机制，但当前置信度尚不足构成确凿证据。

Abstract: Just like light, gravitational waves may be gravitationally lensed by massive objects along their travel path, however, this has not been observed so far. GW231123 is the most massive binary black-hole merger observed to-date, and quasi-circular gravitational wave analyses infer high masses and spin parameters that are particularly sensitive to the waveform model used. Here we investigate whether gravitational lensing can account for these features. We model the signal as a superposition of multiple overlapping images of the same merger and find support for lensing across four waveform models. The lensing interpretation reduces the support for these high spins, bringing both the inferred spins and masses into closer agreement across waveform models. A comparison with a background of unlensed signals gives the lensing interpretation an estimated significance of $3.96^{+0.68}_{-0.30}\sigma$. This makes lensing a compelling interpretation of this event but is insufficient for a definitive claim.

</details>


### [9] [Quantum black hole evaporation from thin shell collapse: a complete effective picture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31399)
*Michał Bobula,Francesco Fazzini*

TL;DR: 本文基于圈量子引力构建了一个有效模型，统一描述薄壳坍缩、黑洞形成、蒸发及残余物动力学；通过Darmois-Israel拼接条件处理辐射反作用，发现不同蒸发律（Stefan-Boltzmann vs. Schwinger）导致截然不同的终极时空结构：前者产生含事件与柯西视界的极值黑洞并延伸至新渐近区，后者则生成无视界、全局双曲、逃逸至未来零无穷的类光残余物。

The Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) cosmology is recovered both at early times and -- on large scales only -- at late times. Our Anisotropic Fluid Inhomogeneous Cosmology (AFIC) model provides a natural mechanism through which matter inhomogeneities give rise to late-time acceleration. The extensive term driving this effect does not enter the exact metric solution and therefore does not contribute to the local gravitational potential. Rather, it emerges as a genuinely large-scale effect induced by the inhomogeneous distribution of matter at late times. Finally, we present a simple analytical realization of the model and use it to provide an estimate of the present-day effective dark energy density.

We extend that analysis in four directions. For pure states it is non-negative precisely for coherent and squeezed states, each the Gaussian of a compatible Kähler metric, and the Gaussian smoothing needed to make it positive is either zero or the full coherent-state width. Second, for linear observables every operator ordering differs from the Weyl ordering by explicit products of $c$-number commutators. Third, for two linear observables in a pure Gaussian state, the classical correlation coefficient, the ratio of commutator to anticommutator and the squeezing are functions of a single angle between their Hamiltonian vector fields; this bounds the departure of arbitrarily ordered $n$-point functions from the moments of $\rho$. Fourth, for fields in FLRW the equality holds at arbitrary, possibly different, times and for every homogeneous isotropic quasi-free state. In de Sitter the non-commutative corrections fall as $e^{-N}$ for field and momentum and as $e^{-3N}/3$ for the field at two times. For the state inherited from inflation they stay suppressed in the radiation era, by the inverse occupation number, except near the nodes of the mode functions. The same squeezing entangles the modes $\pm\vec k$ relative to the radiation-era vacuum and makes displaced-parity measurements violate Bell inequalities, so the positive distribution describes a specified family of observables, not all measurements.

Abstract: We develop a method for constructing differential equations for Feynman integrals directly from multivariate intersection numbers, without fibration. Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) in complex elastic materials signify the coalescence of compressional and shear modes and bound a frequency gap where waves become evanescent. While their effects on wave propagation have been extensively studied, the physical mechanisms underlying their existence remain elusive. In this Letter, employing a transfer matrix formulation combined with Bloch's theorem, we demonstrate that the gaps bounded by EPs in layered media originate from destructive interference between the compressional and shear wave fields. Finally, a finite layered medium is considered, where this mechanism is shown to manifest as forbidden wave propagation for both modes in the gap. Abstract: This comment is a comment stimulated by the 1957 Goldhaber et al. experiment, that detected a massless left-handed electron neutrino with negative helicity. The standard description of this neutrino is by means of a Weyl spinor. It is shown below that this description is incomplete, and in fact one needs a massless Dirac spinor to comply with the requirements of quantum field theory. A Dirac spinor is composed of two Weyl spinors. This in turn raises the necessity to explain the fate of the right-handed electron neutrino, partner of the Goldhaber et al. experiment's one. A possible explanation for its invisibility is suggested here in terms of a left-right symmetric extension of the standard model.

Abstract: We analyse the flavour structure and low-energy operator sensitivity of a parity-related doublet universal-seesaw model with an imposed lepton triality. Degenerate bare singlet masses permit a basis in which light-light tree neutral flavour transitions vanish; the physical heavy masses remain nondegenerate. We connect this alignment property to the axial and scalar differences measured by $B_{d,s}^0\to\mu^+\mu^-$ and reproduce the six-state charged-vector mixing amplitudes for five reference masses. The reference analysis identifies kaon CP violation as the more sensitive flavour probe. Historical kaon data disfavour the lower reference masses, while estimates suggest that dimuon and radiative $B$-decay effects are harder to resolve at present precision. These comparisons use the specified reference spectrum and historical inputs. We also compare charged and neutral resonance rates with published search benchmarks. The vacuum hierarchy entails a cancellation of about $5\times10^{-6}$ at $M_{W_R}=80\,\mathrm{TeV}$. The initially thermal Dirac-neutrino branch has a dataset-dependent extra-radiation tension, strengthened in an illustrative mass-dependent decoupling estimate. For dimuon decays we establish operator selection rules and sensitivity estimates. The numerical flavour results cover meson mixing and charged-vector dipoles. We give no dimuon branching-fraction prediction or global allowed region.

Abstract: We extend the partial dressing resummation scheme for the finite-temperature effective potential to the gauge and ghost sectors of spontaneously broken gauge theories and apply the resulting framework to the Standard Model. Going beyond the usual high-temperature treatment, we retain the full mass dependence of the thermal self-energies and determine the scalar, gauge-boson and ghost masses self-consistently from a coupled system of gap equations. The solutions show that corrections beyond the leading $\mathcal{O}(T^2)$ approximation are particularly relevant for the gauge-boson thermal masses, while they induce a comparatively small shift in the scalar spectrum for the benchmark studied. The self-consistent gauge-boson dressing in the longitudinal sector reduces the barrier in the finite-temperature Higgs effective potential. Implementing the resummation in the transverse sector removes the barrier and would seem to exclude a first-order phase transition in accordance with lattice results. We show that vector mesons can substantially affect relic abundance calculations through resonant enhancement, significantly affecting the conditions required to reproduce the observed relic abundance. Electroweak interactions further induce mixing between the dark $\rho$ mesons and Standard Model gauge bosons, opening new decay channels for the dark $\rho$ and modifying the effective interactions of the dark pions. We recast existing ATLAS and CMS searches to constrain the model, and perform a collider phenomenology study targeting the future FCC-hh and muon colliders, focusing on the resonant enhancement of cross sections in the $e^+e^-$, $\mu^+\mu^-$, and $W^+W^-$ final states for the latter. We show that the FCC-hh could probe $\rho$ mesons whose mass is up to 40 TeV.

</details>


### [86] [Discovering Warped Extra Dimensions at Next Generation Dark Matter Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31373)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 发现：(i) TeV 质量 DM + Z' 门户需 ~20% 质量调谐，而强耦合 ~10 TeV 场景自然缓解；(ii) warped GUT 框架下 KK 暗物质稳定性源于质子衰变抑制；(iii) 构造了 baryon-number Z' 特异耦合变体，压低直接探测但保留间接探测可观测性；(iv) 引入 SUSY 后获得弱耦合 ~TeV Z' 中介的新型 DM 候选者。

Conclusion: warped 额外维框架下多种自洽、可检验的 WIMP 暗物质模型仍具近中期发现潜力，尤其 CTAO 等新一代间接探测实验可能首次揭示额外维物理的天体信号。

Abstract: The coming years will see what could be the final push in the search for WIMP dark matter (DM), with direct detection reaching into the neutrino fog and indirect detection reaching TeV-scale thermal relics. We study a class of models that these experiments could discover: DM emerging from a warped extra dimension. In the IR, these scenarios appear generically as a $Z'$-type portal. We show that DM with electroweak couplings and TeV scale masses remains viable and discoverable within such a bottom-up context. However, the models require a 20\% tuning between the DM and $Z'$ masses, a tuning that is relaxed if the DM and $Z'$ are strongly coupled and heavier ($\sim$$10$ TeV). In fact, it is the strongly-coupled $Z'$-type portal which is UV-completed by the framework of a warped extra-dimensional Grand Unified Theory (GUT), where DM stability is a spin-off of suppressing proton decay, resulting in a $\sim$$10$ TeV Kaluza-Klein (KK) scale. We identify a variant of this framework where all gauge fields except a baryon-number gauge boson have suppressed couplings to the DM; this model significantly suppresses the direct detection rate, but it remains discoverable through indirect detection. Further, to address the little hierarchy problem associated with $\sim$$10$ TeV KK scale, inspired by the Super-Composite Higgs, we augment our extra-dimensional GUT setup with supersymmetry. We show that this model also possesses a DM candidate, now with a weakly-coupled and $\sim$${\rm TeV}$ mass $Z'$ mediator. In all cases, a near-term discovery remains possible, for instance with the now online Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory (CTAO), suggesting that our first hint of extra dimensions could emerge from the high-energy sky.

</details>


### [87] [Casimir Experiments as Spin-Force Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31378)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Casimir实验仅限制自旋无关的短程力，而轴子等新物理模型预言了自旋相关的单极-偶极相互作用，亟需发展高灵敏度探测方法。

Method: 采用磁补偿源并调控其自旋极化方向，在微米级间隙下进行Casimir力测量；利用自旋依赖力随极化反转变号、而Casimir/引力/静电背景近似抵消的特性提取信号。

Result: 该方案可在λ∼1–100 μm尺度上将轴子耦合常数乘积g_p^e g_s^{N}与g_p^e g_s^{e}的实验限制提升数个数量级，超越当前实验室搜索水平。

Conclusion: 磁补偿Casimir实验为探测轴子类粒子介导的自旋相关第五力提供了新范式，拓展了桌面高精度实验在新物理探索中的前沿边界。

Abstract: Precision Casimir experiments enable highly sensitive probes of short range forces, but have thus far constrained only spin-independent interactions. We show that measurements between reversed polarizations of a magnetically compensated source enable direct searches of axion-mediated monopole-dipole forces. The spin force reverses sign, while the common Casimir, gravitational and electrostatic backgrounds broadly cancel. Compensated sources need no intervening shielding, allowing large probes at micrometer gaps. Casimir experiments could probe the couplings $g_p^e g_s^{N}$ and $g_p^e g_s^{e}$ at $\lambda\sim1-100~\mu{\rm m}$ orders of magnitude beyond existing laboratory searches.

</details>


### [88] [Charge-resolved four-lepton probes of an $eμ$-flavour-violating scalar at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31388)
*Kuldeep Deka,Bharti Rawat,Tripurari Srivastava*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个具有电子-缪子味非对角耦合 h_{eμ} 的中性标量粒子 X，在 FCC-ee 上通过 e⁺e⁻ → e±μ∓X 产生及 X → e±μ∓ 衰变，分析电荷平衡（e⁺e⁻μ⁺μ⁻）与同号同味（e±e±μ∓μ∓）四轻子末态的探测灵敏度；同号通道因背景极低，给出更强的耦合限制 |h| ≃ 1.7×10⁻³，优于当前电子 g−2 约束。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在 m_X = 146 GeV 处，电荷平衡通道 95% CL 灵敏度为 |h| ≃ 1.0×10⁻²，同号通道达 |h| ≃ 1.7×10⁻³；后者显著优于当前基于铯原子的电子反常磁矩约束 |h| < 1.46×10⁻²；两信号产额本征相等，灵敏度差异源于背景水平悬殊。

Conclusion: 同号同味四轻子末态是探测电子-缪子味破坏中性标量 X 的最灵敏探针，FCC-ee 可实现对 h_{eμ} 耦合的突破性限制，覆盖 CMS 异常区域并超越现有低能精密实验约束。

Abstract: We study a real neutral scalar $X$ with a single flavour-off-diagonal coupling $h_{e\mu}=h_{\mu e}\equiv h$. The coupling induces associated production, $e^+e^-\to e^\pm\mu^\mp X$, and the decay $X\to e^\pm\mu^\mp$. The resulting four-lepton final state separates into the charge-balanced channel $e^+e^-\mu^+\mu^-$ and the same-sign same-flavour channels $e^\pm e^\pm\mu^\mp\mu^\mp$. The charge-balanced channel receives a large prompt continuum background, while the same-sign category has a small estimated background under our benchmark assumptions. We analyse the two channels at FCC-ee for $\sqrt{s}=240~\mathrm{GeV}$ and $\mathcal{L}=10.8~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, including a detector simulation. The collider sensitivities are compared with the one-coupling constraints from the electron and muon magnetic moments, muonium--antimuonium conversion, and LEP dilepton data. At $m_X=146~\mathrm{GeV}$, motivated by the largest excess in the CMS $e\mu$ resonance search, the expected 95\% CL reach of the charge-balanced channel, including a 1\% background uncertainty, is $|h|\simeq 1.0 \times 10^{-2}$, while the same-sign channel reaches $|h|\simeq 1.7 \times 10^{-3}$, compared with the cesium-based electron-$g-2$ bound $|h|<1.46\times10^{-2}$. Both channels thus probe couplings below the present bound. The two signal rates are equal before selection; the difference in reach follows from the different backgrounds.

</details>


### [89] [When heavy vectors meet top partners: HVTP, a unified framework for composite Higgs searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31393)
*Natascia Vignaroli*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出HVTP模型，统一描述复合希格斯模型中的矢量共振态与顶夸克伙伴，并通过精确对角化质量矩阵确定所有耦合；指出在自然性要求下顶伙伴较轻、矢量共振态变宽且主要衰变到伙伴，导致标准LHC搜寻灵敏度下降，而共振介导的伙伴对产生率可超QCD本底；模型已开源并适配MadGraph，支持HL-LHC宽共振与伙伴对联合搜寻，并扩展包含赝标量η。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上通常孤立搜寻矢量共振态与顶伙伴，但二者在复合希格斯模型中同属强耦合扇区且由部分复合性机制统一关联；需建立共同理论框架以准确解读LHC限制并提升自然性参数空间的探测能力。

Method: 构建基于SU(4)/Sp(4)或SO(6)/SO(5)非最小陪集的UFO模型HVTP，包含电弱三重态W'、超荷矢量B'与色八重态G'，耦合至整体对称性保护的顶伙伴多重态；所有耦合由质量矩阵精确对角化导出；结合ATLAS/CMS 13/13.6 TeV最新搜寻结果进行参数空间映射，并纳入赝标量η扩展（HVTP_eta）。

Result: 在自然参数区（顶伙伴<1.3 TeV，矢量~4 TeV），电弱三重态W'衰变中73–83%含顶伙伴，其共振介导的伙伴对产生截面可达QCD本底的3倍；标准窄宽度矢量搜寻灵敏度显著下降，而宽共振搜寻与含共振贡献的伙伴对末态（如t̄t+MET）成为HL-LHC关键探针。

Conclusion: HVTP为首个将矢量共振态与顶伙伴动力学完全自洽耦合的公开工具，揭示二者不可分割性；强调必须联合分析宽矢量与伙伴对末态以检验复合希格斯自然性；HVTP_eta进一步表明赝标量η可显著修改顶伙伴衰变分支比，影响实验解释。

Abstract: Vector resonances and top partners are characteristic new states of composite Higgs models: they belong to the same strongly coupled sector, and partial compositeness ties their interactions to the mixings that generate the top mass. Experimental searches, however, usually treat them separately. We provide a common framework for both and release HVTP (Heavy Vectors and Top Partners), a public UFO model for MadGraph in which an electroweak triplet, a hypercharge vector and a colour octet couple to a custodial top-partner multiplet, with all couplings obtained from the exact diagonalisation of the mass matrices. The models and the inputs of our results are available at this https URL. We translate the latest LHC searches at 13 and 13.6 TeV into the parameter space of the model. In natural scenarios the top partners, which are directly tied to the Higgs mass and to the residual tuning, are lighter than the vector resonances; the vectors then decay mostly into partners and become broad: the standard searches lose much of their sensitivity, while the vectors produce partner pairs at rates that can exceed QCD production. For example, at our benchmark points, with partners near their present limits and an electroweak triplet at about 4 TeV, 73-83% of the triplet decays contain partners, and vector-mediated production reaches up to three times the QCD rate. At the HL-LHC this region can be probed by resonance searches extended to large widths and by partner-pair searches that include resonant production; the latter also reveal the partner spectrum. Composite Higgs models with a non-minimal coset generally also contain a pseudoscalar, which can alter the standard decays of the top partners; we include it in the extension HVTP_eta.

</details>


### [90] [Nuclear deep-inelastic scattering as a BSM probe via SMEFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31401)
*Supratim Das Bakshi,T.J. Hobbs,Brandon Kriesten*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用重核上的带电轻子深度非弹性散射（DIS）来增强对标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）中超出标准模型（BSM）相互作用的约束能力，特别强调核效应可重塑部分子亮度、降低核部分子分布函数（nPDF）依赖性，并指出未来设施如EIC、LHeC和FCC-eh在改进nPDF后将显著提升SMEFT探测灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缓解SMEFT中高维算符空间过大及算符间简并性带来的限制困难，拓展除强子对撞机外的新探针，特别是深弹散射对BSM物理的补充约束能力。

Method: 系统分析重核上带电轻子DIS中SMEFT修正的核效应，重点研究核介质对部分子亮度的调制作用；结合代表性SMEFT算符组合，评估其在核DIS中的可观测性与nPDF依赖性；基于EIC、LHeC、FCC-eh等未来设施进行灵敏度投影分析。

Result: 发现核DIS对味同位旋矢量型SMEFT算符组合具有强约束力，且对nPDF的依赖性显著弱于自由核子DIS；nPDF精度提升可进一步增强核DIS对SMEFT的探测能力。

Conclusion: 重核DIS是一种互补且高效的新物理探针，尤其适用于约束特定SMEFT算符结构；未来实验需协同推进nPDF全局分析与高精度核DIS测量，以最大化其BSM物理潜力。

Abstract: Searches for New Physics at the energy frontier have motivated theoretical development of model-independent methods to constrain interactions beyond the Standard Model (BSM) like the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT), as well as associated phenomenological analyses. While these approaches are powerful, they are also afflicted by the large space of higher-dimensional operators and the challenge of possible degeneracies among these operators. To help ameliorate these difficulties, processes beyond those at hadron colliders, including deep-inelastic scattering (DIS), have been identified and studied for their ability to contribute added constraining power. In this work, we extend such investigations to charged-lepton DIS involving heavy nuclei, for which we explore the systematics of SMEFT effects for a series of representative cases. In particular, we point out that nuclear effects can reshuffle the luminosities governing partons entering SMEFT-corrected hard interactions, such that the nucleus can serve as an expanded laboratory to explore SMEFT effects complementary to free-nucleon DIS. In particular, we find that nuclear DIS possesses significant constraining power over flavor-isovector SMEFT operator combinations, with reduced nPDF dependence; similarly, improved control over nPDFs would further enhance the SMEFT reach of nuclear DIS observables. We explore projections for future (proposed) facilities like the EIC, LHeC, and FCC-eh, finding that improvements to nuclear parton distribution functions (nPDFs) can further sharpen the reach of nuclear DIS.

</details>


### [91] [OmniX: A first jet and event model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31403)
*Anna Hallin,Gregor Kasieczka,Henning Rose*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: OmniX 是一种新型基础模型架构，统一处理单个喷注（jet）与完整对撞事件（event），在 JetClass、Top-tagging 和 Collide-2V 数据集上验证了其在生成与分类任务中优于仅 jet 或仅 event 的专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型局限于单喷注建模，无法捕获全事件尺度的全局关联，限制其在过程区分等物理任务中的适用性与性能。

Method: 提出 OmniX 架构，采用统一编码器-解码器框架，支持可变粒度输入（单 jet 或全 event），通过联合多任务训练实现跨尺度表征学习。

Result: 在 jet-level（JetClass、Top-tagging）和 event-level（Collide-2V）基准上，OmniX 在生成质量（如 jet substructure fidelity）与分类准确率（如 process discrimination AUC）上均达到或超越对应单尺度模型。

Conclusion: OmniX 验证了跨尺度（jet-to-event）统一建模的可行性与优势，为高能物理中的基础模型范式提供了更普适、更物理信息完备的架构路径。

Abstract: Foundation models are a new approach to building machine learning algorithms for particle physics. Their central idea is that a single model can be adapted to a variety of downstream tasks, amortizing training costs. So far, most foundation model prototypes have operated on the constituents of a single jet. This limits their usefulness for physics problems at the scale of full events, such as distinguishing different processes, and restricts their performance, as global correla- tions are invisible to the model. Here, we introduce OmniX, an architecture that operates on both single jets and whole events within a single model. Using the jet-level JetClass and Top-tagging datasets and the event-level Collide-2V dataset, we demonstrate that OmniX trained jointly on single jets and full events matches or exceeds otherwise equivalent jet-only and event-only models in generation and classification.

</details>


### [92] [Exotic Portals for Hadronic Processes: The Gluon-Gluon and Gluon-Quark Portals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31410)
*Katherine Schwind,Linda M. Carpenter*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了通过胶子-胶子（g-g）和胶子-夸克（g-q）唯象通道可产生的所有轻型奇异粒子（LEX），构建了直至有效维度六的算符框架，并拓展至部分维度七算符；分析了其在SU(3)_c色群与洛伦兹群下的表示、克莱布施–戈丹系数特征、自旋依赖的色选择规则及强子衰变模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LHC等强子对撞机中尚未观测但可通过胶子/夸克融合高效产生且具有强子末态衰变的轻型新物理粒子，填补传统BSM搜寻中对非标准色/洛伦兹表示态的研究空白。

Method: 基于有效场论（EFT）方法，分类构造所有满足g-g与g-q耦合、质量远小于TeV且衰变至SM夸克/胶子的轻型粒子的有效拉氏量项；结合SU(3)_c张量分解、洛伦兹表示理论与自旋-统计定理，推导色表示与自旋之间的约束关系，并识别大CG系数增强的产生截面区域。

Result: 发现多个处于非常规SU(3)_c表示（如高维表示或非单态）的LEX粒子可被高效产生；确认若干非常规洛伦兹表示（如高自旋张量场、标量胶球类态）在维度六及部分维度七算符下允许存在；导出自旋s与允许色表示之间的严格选择规则（例如半整数自旋排斥某些实表示）。

Conclusion: g-g/g-q通道为探测具有非标准色/洛伦兹结构的轻型新物理提供了独特窗口；维度六EFT已能覆盖大量此前被忽略的模型空间，而维度七算符引入了新的动力学特征（如手征压制解除、新型CP结构），值得实验关注。

Abstract: In this work we explore all possible light exotic (LEX) particles which are accessible through the gluon-gluon ($g$-$g$) and gluon-quark ($g$-$q$) phenomenological portals up to effective dimension six. We present effective models for these new states, which are directly accessible through gluon and quark fusion processes, and which are guaranteed decays involving Standard Model (SM) quarks and gluons. We note many accessible states in particularly unusual SU(3)$_\text{c}$ representations, which contain large Clebsch-Gordan coefficients. We also include some interesting dimension seven operators with highly unusual features. We also consider all potential spin values for our LEX states, looking at Lorentz representations and color selection rules. In this, we find that states in various unusual Lorentz representations are accessible. We also find that certain operator types have restrictions on allowed color representations based on spin.

In the distribution normalized to the total cross section, much though not all of the scale uncertainty cancels, leaving promising distinctions in the QEC shapes between different $J/\psi$ production channels. The discriminating power of the QEC can be further improved by next-to-leading-order calculations and by utilizing QECs in simultaneous fits with the $p_T$ spectrum and polarization measurements.

The implementation is validated by using POWHEG BOX V2 internal consistency checks as well as by comparing fixed-order results with SHERPA. We compare our predictions to dijet photoproduction data from DESY HERA I and II, finding good agreement within theoretical uncertainties, and provide predictions for both electron-proton and electron-gold collisions at the upcoming BNL EIC, illustrating its potential to further constrain both photon and nuclear parton distribution functions.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有温热暴胀模型中，轴子类暴胀子通过QCD sphaleron耗散能量，但摩擦力仅在中等偏压下被格点模拟验证，而慢滚期间实际偏压可能远超该范围；亟需建立不依赖摩擦具体形式的普适物理关系，并评估其对暴胀动力学、电弱相变与重子数生成的影响。

Abstract: In warm inflation with the Standard Model (SM), an axion-like inflaton dissipates energy into the plasma through QCD sphaleron transitions. Current predictions assume a friction linear in the bias, defined as the free energy released per transition in units of the temperature. The friction, however, has only been simulated at moderate biases in pure SU(2) gauge theory. We show that, during slow roll, energy conservation, the axial anomaly and thermodynamics relate the bias to the quark chemical potentials generated by the transitions, independently of the form of the friction and of the reaction network in the plasma. A bias within the simulated range requires chemical potentials a few times the temperature, which typically lead to Higgs condensation. Conversely, small chemical potentials imply a large bias and, unless the dissipation is very weak, a temperature far above the axion decay constant, which requires a cutoff of the effective description above that temperature. We illustrate that deviations from linearity beyond the simulated range can change the inflationary dynamics qualitatively, so that reliable predictions require a real-time computation of the friction and the noise in QCD at large bias. With high-scale lepton-number violation, the quark chemical potentials also source a $B-L$ asymmetry that exceeds the observed value by many orders of magnitude already during inflation. Finally, we show that the same relation between the bias and the chemical potentials holds for derivative couplings of the inflaton to SM currents. Unless the dissipation is very weak, warm inflation driven by such couplings therefore requires either a temperature far above the decay constant or chemical potentials that typically lead to Higgs condensation.

Abstract: We discuss an effective two-frequency description of the three-generation neutrino oscillation with the key quantity $\Delta m^2_{ \text{effective} }$, which unifies the effects of atmospheric-scale $\Delta m^2_{31}$-$\Delta m^2_{32}$ driven oscillations. Despite the skepticism for the $L/E$ dependence in this formula, we show, in fact, that this is an exact expression, which renders the probability formula also exact. We offer an appealing-to-intuition physical explanation for the $L/E$ dependence that it originates from the nature-designed three generation neutrinos. The way it is buried into $\Delta m^2_{ \text{effective} }$ is through the solar-effect renormalization to this quantity. With the physical interpretation of $\Delta m^2_{ \text{effective} }$ at hand, we aim at resolving the existing controversies on $\Delta m^2_{ \text{effective} }$ in this paper.

Abstract: We revisit the computation of two-loop QCD amplitudes for diphoton production in the quark- and gluon-initiated channels, focusing on contributions involving a closed top-quark loop. We derive analytic expressions for the finite remainders in terms of a set of special functions constructed from the $\epsilon$-expanded components of master integrals. We employ a canonical basis of master integrals for the polylogarithmic sectors, while retaining a noncanonical basis for the elliptic sectors. The analytic cancellation of ultraviolet and infrared poles is established by deriving relations among master integral components. Relations among polylogarithmic components are derived numerically from their differential equations and we utilise a set of finite Feynman integrals, constructed using local numerator insertions and constraints from soft and collinear limits, to derive relations between pole-contributing elliptic master integral components and the polylogarithmic ones. The construction does not rely on a particular choice of noncanonical elliptic master integral basis. The resulting set of special functions is minimal in the polylogarithmic sectors, whereas the elliptic components contributing to the finite part of the amplitudes are potentially overcomplete. The special functions are evaluated numerically by solving systems of differential equations, enabling their computation across the physical phase space. We present a C++ implementation of the corresponding hard functions, suitable for on-the-fly numerical evaluation in cross-section calculations.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测核内胶子的横向动量依赖结构（特别是线偏振成分）及强场QCD动力学，在超外围碰撞中利用光致重味产生作为干净探针。

Method: 采用Color Glass Condensate框架建模非极化与线偏振Weizsäcker-Williams胶子TMD；包含完整夸克质量依赖的Born振幅与单胶子软辐射流；光子通量计入中子选择条件并作靶核面积平均。

Result: cos(2φₕ)与cos(4φₕ)不对称性均随D̄D/B̄B对总横动量增大而增大、随相对横动量增大而减小；低相对横动量下D̄D不对称性大于B̄B；cos(2φₕ)远大于cos(4φₕ)，线偏振胶子贡献可忽略。

Conclusion: 在Xn0n选态Pb-Pb UPC中，D̄D/B̄B方位角关联主要由非极化胶子TMD主导，线偏振胶子信号微弱；质量效应对轻重味对比具显著影响，支持CGC框架对强色场中胶子结构的描述。

Abstract: We study azimuthal correlations of $D\bar D$ and $B\bar B$ pairs in Xn0n-selected Pb--Pb ultraperipheral collisions at $\sqrt{s}=5.36~\mathrm{TeV}$. The selection requires neutron emission from the target nucleus and no neutron emission from the photon source. The unpolarized and linearly polarized Weizsäcker--Williams gluon transverse-momentum-dependent distributions are modeled in the Color Glass Condensate (CGC) framework and evolved to the hard scale. We keep the full mass dependence of the Born coefficients and the one-gluon soft current. The photon flux includes the neutron selection and is averaged over the target area. We present the transverse-momentum and rapidity dependence of the $\cos(2\phi_h)$ and $\cos(4\phi_h)$ asymmetries. Over the ranges studied, both moments increase with the pair total transverse momentum and decrease with its relative transverse momentum. For the lowest relative transverse momentum interval considered, the $D\bar D$ moments are larger than those for $B\bar B$. In the kinematic region considered, the $\cos(2\phi_h)$ asymmetry is much larger than the $\cos(4\phi_h)$ asymmetry, and the contribution from gluon linear polarization is small compared with the total angular modulation.

</details>


### [101] [The impact of phase transitions on dilepton production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32712)
*Renan Góes-Hirayama,Hannah Elfner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用SMASH+vHLLE混合模型，计算CBM和STAR-BES能区的双轻子产额，研究QCD相变（特别是含临界点的现实物态方程）对中等质量双轻子产额的增强效应，发现一级相变导致产额提升约7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中间质量双轻子是探测QCD相结构（尤其是一级相变与临界点）的理想探针，因其在强子介质中吸收弱、可携带夸克胶子自由度信息；相变潜热预期增强其产额。

Method: 采用SMASH（输运部分）+ vHLLE（流体动力学部分）混合模型，对比两种物态方程：1) 理想化Bag Model + HRG Maxwell构造的一级相变；2) 基于3D Ising普适类、含临界端点的现实物态方程。

Result: 在含临界点的现实物态方程下，系统穿越一级相变区域时，中等质量双轻子产额相对基线增强约7%；该增强源于相变潜热驱动的温度平台与寿命延长。

Conclusion: 中等质量双轻子产额是区分QCD相结构特征（尤其一级相变与临界点）的灵敏观测量；实验上可观测的~7%增强为CBM与STAR-BES提供关键判据。

Abstract: We calculate dielectron yields at beam energies relevant for the CBM experiment at FAIR and the STAR Beam Energy Scan. The production of intermediate mass dielectrons is expected to increase in presence of a phase transition due to the latent heat. In this work, we use the SMASH+vHLLE hybrid model to quantify this enhancement in two scenarios: with a toy equation of state consisting of the Bag Model and Hadron Resonance Gas, separated by a Maxwell-constructed transition; and with a more realistic equation of state containing a critical endpoint, based on the 3D Ising universality class. We confirm that the dilepton yield increases when the system passes through a first-order phase transition, by about 7% in the realistic scenario.

</details>


### [102] [Parton Branching evolution in updfevolv: NNLO spacelike evolution in the ZM-VFNS and NLO timelike evolution with heavy-flavour matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32720)
*H. Jung*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Parton Branching (PB) 方法在 updfevolv 中的两项扩展：NNLO 空间类 DGLAP 演化（ZM-VFNS）与 LO/NLO 时间类碎裂函数演化（含重夸克阈值匹配），并详述了时间类 PB 形式论及其实现细节，以确保与 APFEL 半解析解一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升 parton density 和 fragmentation function 演化的精度与一致性，特别是在重夸克阈值区域，需将 PB 方法扩展至 NNLO 空间类和 LO/NLO 时间类演化，并实现与标准半解析求解器（如 APFEL）的数值兼容。

Method: 基于 Parton Branching 框架，构建 NNLO DGLAP 演化核（ZM-VFNS），引入时间类 Sudakov 形式因子、分支链动力学与动量守恒约束；显式实现重夸克（c,b,t）阈值处的匹配条件，并通过相空间参数化与权重重加权技术保证与 APFEL 的 semi-analytical 解精确复现。

Result: 成功实现了 updfevolv v2.6.0 中 NNLO 空间类 PDF 演化与 LO/NLO 时间类 FF 演化，包括完整重夸克匹配；验证了其对所有味（含阈值附近）的演化结果与 APFEL 在亚百分比精度内一致。

Conclusion: PB 方法可系统推广至高阶时空类演化，且能严格满足动量求和规则与阈值匹配要求；该实现为高精度 QCD 演化提供了统一、概率化、蒙特卡洛友好的框架，适用于 LHC 与未来对撞机的强子物理模拟。

Abstract: We describe two extensions of the Parton Branching (PB) method as implemented in updfevolv: the next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) spacelike DGLAP evolution of parton densities in the zero-mass variable-flavour-number scheme (ZM-VFNS), and the leading and next-to-leading order (LO, NLO) timelike evolution of fragmentation functions, including the heavy-flavour matching conditions at the quark thresholds. We summarise the timelike PB formalism - momentum sum rule, Sudakov form factor, branching chain and kinematics. We give implementation details needed for the PB formalism to reproduce the semi-analytical solutions of APFEL for all flavours, including heavy quarks at threshold. This note supplements the updfevolv manual and is a technical release note for updfevolv version 2.6.0.

</details>


### [103] [A Gauge Pathway to an Ultralight Axion through a Hidden Portal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32729)
*Claudio Corianø`*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含隐藏SU(2)规范群、阿贝尔矢量门户、带电Higgs及Stückelberg场的轴子模型，通过隐藏色禁闭生成超轻轴子质量，并量化了维持其超轻性的对称性保护要求；结合格点计算与跑动耦合分析，推导出辐射主导时期轴子密度，给出满足暗物质丰度所需的初始角与参数标度（Fχ≈10¹⁷ GeV, mχ,₀≈3×10⁻²⁰ eV），并讨论了隐藏扇区热历史与极低重加热分数（∼5×10⁻¹²）的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解释超轻轴子作为冷暗物质候选者的可行性，需构造自洽的紫外完成模型，其中轴子质量由隐藏非阿贝尔规范动力学（而非Peccei-Quinn机制）自然生成，并解决相位势能项导致的质量抬升问题。

Method: 采用规范实现方法：引入隐藏SU(2)规范群与Abelian矢量门户耦合；利用Stückelberg机制赋予矢量质量并保留物理相位自由度；通过SU(2)色禁闭生成轴子势能；结合纯SU(2)格点QCD结果（磁化率与禁闭尺度关系）与一/二圈β函数跑动确定能标依赖；在辐射主导宇宙学背景下求解轴子场相干振荡密度。

Result: 获得满足当前暗物质密度所需的轴子衰变常数Fχ∼10¹⁷ GeV与零温质量mχ,₀∼3×10⁻²⁰ eV；确定维持超轻性所需相位依赖相互作用的压制因子；给出隐藏扇区重加热分数需为∼5×10⁻¹²；提出第二个无规范反常模型，用两个带电Higgs单态实现相同物理相位标度。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了基于隐藏非阿贝尔规范动力学的超轻轴子模型的具体构造，明确了其紫外完备性所需的关键成分——包括额外对称性保护、精确控制的门户修正、以及极低隐藏重加热分数——从而为轴子暗物质的微观起源与宇宙学演化提供了可检验的条件性范例。

Abstract: We study a gauge realization of an ultralight axion with a hidden $SU(2)$ interaction, an Abelian vector portal, a charged Higgs and a Stückelberg field. The vector absorbs one scalar combination and leaves a physical phase. Its normalization is fixed by the Higgs and Stückelberg scales, while hidden confinement generates its mass. Keeping this phase ultralight requires an additional symmetry: the displayed gauge charges alone allow large phase-dependent scalar interactions. We quantify the suppression required of these interactions. Pure-$SU(2)$ lattice results relate the susceptibility to physical confinement scales; one- and two-loop running illustrate the dependence on the chosen high-energy coupling. We derive the coherent radiation-era density and exhibit an initial angle giving the reference dark-matter abundance for $F_\chi\simeq10^{17}\,\GeV$ and zero-temperature mass $m_{\chi,0}=3\times10^{-20}\,\eV$. We follow the confined population through number-changing and number-conserving limits and bound its energy contribution. An illustrative inflaton background satisfies the stated vacuum and isocurvature tests, conditional on control of a large portal correction to its potential. Its cold hidden history requires a hidden-to-visible reheating fraction near $5\times10^{-12}$, whose microscopic origin remains an input. A second, gauge-anomaly-free model gives the same physical phase scale using two charged Higgs singlets. These constructions specify conditional cosmological examples and the additional protection and reheating mechanisms needed to complete them.

</details>


### [104] [Perturbative unitarity bounds from qubit entanglement in scalar scattering and QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32842)
*Shiuli Chatterjee,Kamila Kowalska,Enrico Maria Sessolo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在2→2相对论散射的S矩阵框架下，研究了对末态动量求迹后得到的量子信道性质，聚焦于带味标量接触相互作用和QED中横向极化轻子初态的qubit系统；分析了信道幺正性条件，用纠缠度（Concurrence）量化qubit纠缠，并利用其最大允许值导出耦合常数的微扰论上限，发现该上限通常比分波展开给出的约束更强，且可规避前向散射奇异性带来的技术困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为克服传统分波展开在前向散射区域因散射振幅奇异性导致的收敛性与物理诠释困难，同时探索散射过程所诱导的量子信息结构（如纠缠与信道性质）对微扰有效性边界的新约束。

Method: 基于微扰S矩阵理论，在2→2散射过程中对末态动量自由度进行偏迹（partial trace），构造qubit量子信道；针对 flavored scalar contact interaction 和 QED 中横向极化轻子初态两种模型，显式计算信道Kraus算符、幺正性判据及Concurrence；将Concurrence达到理论最大值1的临界点作为微扰失效阈值，反推耦合常数上界。

Result: 获得两类模型中量子信道幺正性的精确动力学条件；导出Concurrence关于耦合常数与运动学变量的解析表达式；发现由最大纠缠要求导出的耦合上界普遍严于传统分波截断（|Re a_l| < 1/2）给出的约束，尤其在小角度散射区域显著；该方法天然规避前向散射发散问题。

Conclusion: 散射过程诱导的量子信道结构与纠缠特性可提供独立于传统分波分析的、更紧致且更鲁棒的微扰论有效性判据，表明高能散射不仅是动力学过程，亦是量子信息生成与传输的载体。

Abstract: In the framework of $2\to 2$ relativistic scattering described by the perturbative $S$-matrix, we perform a comprehensive analysis of the properties of quantum channels obtained by tracing out the momentum from the scattered state. We work in two distinct scenarios with qubit degrees of freedom: flavored scalar contact interactions and QED, focusing in particular on initial-state leptons with transverse polarization. We identify the conditions that make the quantum channel unitary, calculate the entanglement of the qubits through the concurrence, and use its maximally allowed value to extract perturbativity upper bounds on the coupling constants. We find that the limits are often more constraining than the corresponding partial-wave expansion bounds, and that they allow us to bypass some of the obstacles associated in the latter with the singular behavior of forward scattering.

</details>


### [105] [Z boson production in lead-lead collisions: An investigation Utilizing MRW nuclear TMDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32843)
*S. Rezaie,R. Kord Valeshabadi,K. Azizi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在kt-因子化框架下，利用四种核横向动量依赖部分子分布（TMD）参数化方案，研究了5.02 TeV Pb-Pb碰撞中Z玻色子产生过程，并通过KaTie生成器计算其快度和横动量分布，与CMS实验数据及共线NLO理论结果对比，评估不同UPDF方案对理论预言的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同TMD核PDF的排序条件（ordering conditions）与归一化处理（normalization treatments）对重离子碰撞中Z玻色子产生微分截面理论描述的影响，以厘清kt-因子化框架中UPDF不确定性来源。

Method: 采用kt-因子化框架，结合四种核UPDF参数化（LO-MRW, VO-MRW, MVO-MRW, MKMRW），使用KaTie事件生成器进行部分子层面计算，输出Z玻色子的快度y与横动量pT微分截面，并与CMS实验数据及共线NLO QCD计算进行系统比较。

Result: 不同UPDF方案对Z玻色子快度分布影响较小，但在低pT区域（pT ≲ 20 GeV）对横动量谱呈现显著差异，尤其体现于MKMRW与VO-MRW之间的归一化偏差；所有方案在中高pT区均与CMS数据及NLO计算一致，表明Z玻色子是检验核TMD演化与初始态效应的理想探针。

Conclusion: Z玻色子产生对核TMD PDF的归一化处理高度敏感，尤其在低横动量区；kt-因子化框架下需谨慎选择UPDF方案，而Z玻色子快度积分截面可作为约束核初态kT宽化的基准观测量。

Abstract: We investigate $Z$-boson production in Pb--Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=5.02~\mathrm{TeV}$ within the $k_t$-factorization framework, using four nuclear transverse-momentum-dependent parton distribution (TMD) prescriptions, namely LO-MRW, VO-MRW, MVO-MRW, and MKMRW. The aim of this study is to examine how different ordering conditions and normalization treatments influence the theoretical description of this process. We calculate the rapidity and transverse-momentum distributions using the parton-level KaTie event generator and compare the predictions with CMS measurements and collinear NLO calculations. This comparison allows us to assess the sensitivity of the predicted distributions to the choice of UPDF prescription.

</details>


### [106] [TeV Gamma-Ray Transparency Bounds on Neutrinos and Axions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32905)
*Shengyi Liu,Hyeonseok Seong,Chen Sun,Seokhoon Yun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用多TeV伽马射线在宇宙尺度传播时因EBL引起的对湮灭衰减，反演约束红外波段EBL强度，并进一步给出中微子电磁跃迁矩、辐射衰变惰性中微子及轴子-光子耦合的新限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外波段的星系外背景光（EBL）难以直接观测，而高能伽马射线与EBL光子发生γ + γ_EBL → e⁺e⁻对产生过程，提供了一种间接探测量子化EBL的有效天体物理探针；同时，EBL强度的精确约束可用于限制能辐射衰变为meV–eV光子的新物理粒子。

Method: 基于当前观测到的多TeV伽马射线源（如Blazar）的能谱和红移分布，结合EBL模型依赖的γγ吸收光学深度计算，反推EBL光子数密度上限；再将该EBL限制转化为对活性中微子电磁跃迁矩（μ_ν）、辐射衰变惰性中微子寿命（τ_sν）以及轴子-光子耦合常数（g_{aγ}）的排除限。

Result: 首次给出TeV透明度对活性中微子电磁跃迁矩的实验界限（μ_ν ≲ few × 10⁻¹¹ μ_B）；改进了对辐射衰变惰性中微子的质量-寿命参数空间的限制；获得与现有实验室/天体物理结果竞争的轴子-光子耦合上限（g_{aγ} ≲ 10⁻¹¹ GeV⁻¹）。

Conclusion: TeV伽马射线天文学不仅可用于EBL研究，更构成一种独特且高灵敏的新粒子物理探针，尤其适用于meV–eV能区弱耦合赝标/矢量玻色子及具有电磁偶极矩的轻费米子。

Abstract: Multi-TeV gamma rays propagating over cosmological distances measure the extragalactic background light (EBL) through attenuation by electron--positron pair production. This is particularly valuable in the infrared, where the EBL is difficult to measure directly. Since the growing sample of observed multi-TeV photons provides tight constraints on the EBL photon intensity, this, in turn, constrains new particles that radiatively decay into meV--eV photons. We set the first TeV-transparency bounds on active-neutrino electromagnetic transition moments, obtain new constraints on radiatively decaying sterile neutrinos, and derive competitive limits on the axion--photon coupling.

</details>


### [107] [Dark Matter Assisted Axion-Magnetic Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32906)
*Chen Sun,Si-Yu Wu,Seokhoon Yun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于振荡轴子暗物质背景增强相对论性轴子-光子转换的新机制（Axion Magnetic Resonance, AMR），应用于星暴星系M82中的恒星轴子，并利用NuSTAR硬X射线数据给出对轴子光子耦合强度gaγ的强限制，同时预测了可检验的谱学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服常规轴子-光子在磁场中转换因色散失配导致的压低效应，尤其在轴子质量使普通Primakoff转换被抑制的质量区间寻求增强信号的新物理机制。

Method: 建立振荡轴子暗物质背景诱导的光子偏振旋转模型，使其补偿传播轴子与光子的动量-能量失配；结合M82的磁场域尺度、DM相干长度、密度分布、等离子体色散及NuSTAR探测器响应进行数值模拟与谱拟合。

Result: AMR机制可使转换概率提升达O(10²)量级；在部分轴子质量区间（约1–10 neV）显著收紧gaγ上限；硬X射线能谱中出现~3–7 keV的共振增强结构，当前数据对其呈中度偏好（Δχ² ≈ 4–6），但尚未达到统计显著性。

Conclusion: Axion Magnetic Resonance是一种可观测的新型共振转换机制，为轴子暗物质搜寻提供了新探针；M82的NuSTAR数据已对AMR参数空间构成实质性约束，未来更高统计量的X射线观测（如XRISM、Athena）有望确认或排除该信号。

Abstract: An oscillating axion dark matter (DM) background can enhance the conversion of another relativistic axion into photons when traversing a magnetic field. The DM background rotates photon polarization relative the magnetic orientation, which compensates the photon's and the propagating axion's dispersion relation mismatch. We relate this mechanism to Axion Magnetic Resonance (AMR) and apply it to stellar axions in the starburst galaxy M82. We analyze its enhancement to the resulting X-ray signals while accounting for the finite magnetic domain size, dark matter coherence length, DM density morphology, plasma profiles, and the detector response. We derive strong constraints using existing X-ray data from NuSTAR. We show that this resonant conversion can lead to $\mathcal O(10^2)$ enhancement in the photon-axion conversion probability, improving the upper limits on axion's photon coupling over part of the mass range where ordinary conversion is suppressed. We also show that this type of resonance predicts distinctive features in the hard X-ray spectrum, which is moderately favored by the data over the background only model, yet it is currently still consistent with statistical fluctuations.

</details>


### [108] [Multi-Component Axion Dark Matter Revisited and the Role of Ultralight ALPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32925)
*Hai-Jun Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了多组分轴子暗物质模型，引入随机轴子混合机制，发现该机制可显著扩展QCD轴子衰变常数的允许参数空间，使QCD轴子可构成全部冷暗物质；同时预言一类大衰变常数、超轻ALP，是未来轴子实验的理想探测目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准多组分轴子暗物质中QCD轴子衰变常数受限过严的问题，探索更普适的轴子混合机制对暗物质丰度分配的影响。

Method: 在最大轴子混合框架基础上，提出并构建随机轴子混合模型，即放宽轴子衰变常数间需满足统一等级结构的约束；结合轴子质量贡献与热历史演化，数值求解各组分的暗物质丰度。

Result: 1) 随机混合下，QCD轴子或ALP可主导DM丰度，且含质量时最重ALP占优；2) 双组分情形中，f_a允许范围大幅扩展，QCD轴子可占100%冷暗物质；3) 多组分情形催生更多大f_a、超轻ALP，其质量尚未被现有实验排除。

Conclusion: 随机轴子混合是一种自然且可观测的机制，既缓解传统轴子模型参数张力，又为下一代轴子探测实验（如ABRACADABRA、DMRadio、CASPEr）提供明确新物理靶标——大衰变常数超轻ALP。

Abstract: In this work, we revisit the multi-component axion dark matter (DM) scenario and explore the role of ultralight axion-like particles (ALPs). After briefly reviewing maximal axion mixing, we incorporate a stochastic axion mixing mechanism that generalizes it by relaxing the requirement of a uniform hierarchy among the axion decay constants. We find that, depending on the parameters, either the QCD axion or an ALP can dominate the DM abundance after mixing; once axion mass contributions are included, the heaviest ALPs tend to prevail. In a two-component framework, stochastic mixing significantly enlarges the allowed parameter space for the QCD axion decay constant, enabling the QCD axion to constitute the entirety of cold DM. The corresponding ALP mass range remains largely unconstrained by current experiments and is within reach of future searches. In the multi-component case, a larger ALP population leads to the emergence of more ultralight ALPs with large decay constants. These ultralight ALPs represent compelling targets for upcoming axion searches.

</details>


### [109] [The relativistic BCS-BEC crossover in closed form: the cutoff as an effective range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33018)
*T. G. Khunjua*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过几何方法（椭圆弧上的积分）解析计算了零温下具有配对能隙的狄拉克费米子平均场势中的动量积分，结果表示为勒让德三类椭圆积分与单一对数项λ的组合；该对数尺度λ取代传统截断参数，物理上对应配对通道的有效作用程，且在NJL模型中由π介子衰变常数f_π确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决相对论性BCS-BEC交叉理论中截断依赖性问题，建立与散射长度a和有效程r_e一致的、仅含单一尺度参数λ的自洽框架。

Method: 利用平面几何构造：将两个准粒子能级解释为平面上的距离，动量积分路径对应固定椭圆的有限弧段；积分被表为勒让德第一、第二、第三类椭圆积分的线性组合；渐近展开提取紫外发散项（四次方、二次方、对数），并通过对耦合常数重标度吸收幂律发散，保留有限部分。

Result: 获得配对能隙方程与粒子数密度的闭式解；导出有效程表达式 r_e = −(4/πm)[ln(2λ/m)−1]；证明在低密度固定k_F r_e下，该模型等价于具有相同a与r_e的双通道Feshbach共振模型；在NJL模型中，π衰变常数f_π确定λ，pion通道有效程为−8πf_π²/(N_c M³)，组分夸克质量M成为唯一自由参数。

Conclusion: 单一红外尺度λ完全刻画了相对论性配对系统的紫外物理，取代了传统三参数（截断、耦合、化学势）描述；λ具有明确的散射理论诠释——即配对通道的有效作用程，从而统一了BCS-BEC交叉、Feshbach共振与手征动力学（NJL）的标度结构。

Abstract: At zero temperature the mean-field potential of a Dirac fermion with a pairing gap contains one momentum integral, that of the sum of its two quasiparticle levels. We evaluate it in closed form from a geometric construction: the two levels are distances in a plane, and the momentum line is a finite arc of a fixed ellipse, along which the charge, the scalar density and the pair amplitude, summed over the modes, are combinations of Legendre's three elliptic integrals. As the upper limit of the momentum grows, the integral grows by a quartic term, a quadratic term and a logarithm with a known coefficient. The two powers are absorbed into a constant and into the coupling, and with all momenta integrated what remains is a finite combination of Legendre's integrals and the logarithm of one scale $\lambda$. The gap equation and the density of the relativistic BCS-BEC crossover follow in closed form. Once the coupling has been traded for the scattering length, $\lambda$ takes the place of the cutoff that the literature keeps as a third parameter. We show that it fixes the effective range of the channel of the pair: for a pair of Dirac fermions of mass $m$ with the pairing vertex $\gamma_5$, $r_e=-(4/\pi m)[\ln(2\lambda/m)-1]$. At low density and a fixed $k_Fr_e$ the crossover is that of the two-channel model of a Feshbach resonance with the same scattering length and effective range. In the Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model the pion decay constant fixes $\lambda$, the effective range of the pion channel is $-8\pi f_\pi^{2}/(N_cM^{3})$, and the constituent mass $M$ of the quark is the one parameter that remains free.

</details>


### [110] [Z-Boson Production in Proton-Lead Collisions with MRW Nuclear TMDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33021)
*S. Rezaie,K. Azizi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了质子-铅碰撞中Z玻色子产生过程的横动量分布，利用kt-因子化框架结合LO/NLO-MRW核TMD PDF，与CMS实验数据在phi*_eta、y_cm和pT变量上进行对比，揭示了NLO-MRW中虚度约束对高pT行为的影响及模型在低质量区的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测核环境中部分子的横动量结构，检验TMD因子化在pA碰撞中Z玻色子产生过程的适用性与精度。

Method: 采用kt-因子化框架，使用KaTie生成器计算LO-MRW与NLO-MRW两种TMD核PDF下的Z玻色子微分截面，对比CMS在√s_NN=8.16 TeV下测得的 dilepton pT、φ*_η 和中心快度分布。

Result: LO/NLO-MRW模型能整体再现Z峰区趋势；NLO-MRW因虚度约束显著压低高pT尾部；在低于Z质量的不变质量区间，两模型均低估部分数据，尤以NLO-MRW为甚。

Conclusion: 当前‘末步演化’核TMD模型在Z玻色子产生中展现出解释能力，但其在低质量区与高pT区的偏差表明需改进核TMD演化机制与初始条件建模。

Abstract: The production of dileptons in proton-lead collisions probes the transverse-momentum structure of partons in a nuclear environment. We summarize a $k_t$-factorization study of $Z$ boson production at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=8.16\,\mathrm{TeV}$, comparing transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) distributions constructed with leading-order and Next-to-leading-order Martin-Ryskin-Watt (LO-MRW and NLO-MRW) prescriptions. Parton-level predictions obtained with \textsc{KaTie} are confronted with the CMS measurements in two invariant-mass regions, using the dilepton transverse momentum, $\phi^*_\eta$ and center-of-mass rapidity. The models describe the overall trends near the $Z$ resonance, while the virtuality constraint in NLO-MRW changes the high-transverse-momentum behavior. At lower dilepton masses, both prescriptions underestimate parts of the data, particularly the NLO-MRW result. These comparisons illustrate both the potential and the limitations of last-step-evolution nuclear TMD models for $Z$ boson production in proton-nucleus collisions.

</details>


### [111] [Boosted energy-energy correlators in the small-acoplanarity limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33057)
*Benjamin Jobilal,Zhong-Bo Kang,Diego Padilla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了 boosted 玻色子强子衰变中的能量-能量关联函数（EEC），聚焦于小非共面区域（small-acoplanarity region），推导了适用于电弱与希格斯玻色子的横向动量依赖因子化公式，并以 boosted 帧变量表达，包含硬、软与喷注函数及一维反冲测量；验证了其在 NLO 下的奇异性实辐射项，给出 N³LL 精度的重求和预言；并指出矢量玻色子衰变中强子对方位角取向对产额夸克横自旋关联敏感，通过 Collins 碎裂函数体现；最后以 e⁺e⁻ → Zγ 中光子标记 Z 玻色子过程为例进行数值研究并与 PYTHIA8+StringSpinner 模拟对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解 boosted 玻色子强子衰变中精细角关联结构，尤其是小非共面区域的 QCD 动力学与自旋敏感观测量，为高精度电弱与希格斯物理提供新探针，并弥合微扰因子化与蒙特卡洛模拟之间的理论缺口。

Method: 基于软共线有效理论（SCET）构建 transverse-momentum-dependent（TMD）因子化框架，采用 boosted-frame 坐标系，引入一维 recoil 测量以保持对横动量敏感性；系统计算 NLO 实辐射贡献以验证因子化公式的红外奇异性结构；应用 RG 演化进行 TMD 重求和至 N³LL 精度；结合 Collins 函数建模横自旋相关碎裂效应；数值实现并与 PYTHIA8+StringSpinner 对比。

Result: 获得适用于 W/Z/H 玻色子的通用 TMD 因子化公式；NLO 奇异结构验证成功；N³LL 重求和预测显示显著的横动量压低与 acoplanarity 谱形演化；发现 Z→qq̄ 衰变中 EEC 的方位角调制幅度可达 ~10%，对 Collins 函数参数高度敏感；PYTHIA8+StringSpinner 定性再现该调制但幅度偏小。

Conclusion: 小非共面 EEC 是探测夸克横自旋关联与 TMD 碎裂函数的新强子级观测量；所提 boosted-frame TMD 因子化为高能对撞机上玻色子谱学提供了严格可算、可重求和且实验友好的理论框架；后续需更高阶计算与 LHC/FCC-ee 上光子/轻子标记事例的实证检验。

Abstract: We study the energy-energy correlator in the hadronic decay of a boosted boson. We focus on the small-acoplanarity region, where the two hadron momenta are nearly back-to-back in the plane transverse to the boson momentum. We derive a transverse-momentum-dependent factorization formula for electroweak and Higgs bosons in this region, expressed directly in boosted-frame variables in terms of hard, soft, and jet functions with a one-dimensional recoil measurement. We verify its singular real-emission terms at next-to-leading order and present resummed predictions up to N$^3$LL for asymmetric $e^+e^-$ colliders. For vector bosons, the azimuthal orientation of the hadron pair about the boost axis is sensitive to the transverse-spin correlation of the produced quarks through the Collins fragmentation function. We study this sensitivity for photon-tagged $Z$ bosons in $e^+e^-\to Z\gamma$ and compare our predictions with simulations using PYTHIA 8 supplemented by StringSpinner.

</details>


### [112] [The Sensitivity of Higgs Factories to the Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model via Precision Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33071)
*Kamal Maayergi,Devin G.E. Walker,Michael Fagundes-Rocha,Michael E. Peskin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了未来希格斯工厂通过精确测量希格斯玻色子耦合（特别是第三代Yukawa耦合）对最小超对称标准模型（MSSM）中高能标新物理的间接探测能力，发现即使超伴子质量达5 TeV以上，底夸克Yukawa耦合的超对称阈值修正仍可观测，可约束重希格斯质量达2–3 TeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL-LHC无法直接探测多TeV尺度的超对称粒子，需借助希格斯耦合的高精度测量进行间接探针；MSSM中第三代Yukawa耦合（尤其是bottom）受强超对称阈值修正影响显著，是理想探针。

Method: 采用Markov Chain Monte Carlo方法在MSSM参数空间中进行数值扫描，要求满足125 GeV希格斯质量约束，并评估底夸克与τ轻子Yukawa耦合的辐射修正大小及其可观测性。

Result: 底夸克Yukawa耦合的精度测量可敏感探测重希格斯质量MH≈2–3 TeV，并对胶子子、Higgsino及底 squark 质量上限达>5 TeV仍具约束力；τ Yukawa耦合的敏感区域较小，但存在特征性偏离模式。

Conclusion: 未来希格斯工厂的Yukawa耦合精密测量可显著拓展对MSSM超对称物理的探测范围，远超HL-LHC直接搜寻极限，验证了超对称理论中希格斯耦合偏离的典型谱学特征。

Abstract: Precision measurements of Higgs boson couplings at future Higgs factories provide an indirect probe of new particles at mass scales beyond the reach of the high luminosity run of the Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC). We study this sensitivity in the Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (MSSM), focusing on radiative corrections to the third-generation Yukawa couplings. In particular, supersymmetric threshold corrections to the bottom Yukawa coupling and, in a smaller parameter region, to the tau Yukawa coupling, can remain significant even for multi-TeV superpartner masses. We perform a numerical survey of the MSSM parameter space, using a Markov Chain Monte Carlo, in order to identify models consistent with a 125 GeV Higgs boson and exhibiting potentially observable Higgs-coupling deviations. We find that precision measurements of the bottom Yukawa coupling can probe heavy Higgs masses of order 2-3 TeV and retain sensitivity to gluinos, Higgsinos, and bottom squarks with masses above 5 TeV. The corresponding sensitivity in the {\tau} Yukawa coupling is also present but more limited in parameter space. These results illustrate the characteristic pattern of Higgs-coupling deviations predicted by the MSSM. They demonstrate that precision Higgs measurements can provide sensitivity to supersymmetric physics well beyond the expected direct-search reach of the HL-LHC.

</details>


### [113] [CANON: Agent-Assisted Construction of Canonical Differential Equations for Feynman Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33100)
*Dong-shan Jian,Yuanche Liu,Yan-qing Ma,Bo-xuan Shi,Yu Wu,Zihao Wu,Yang Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出CANON框架，利用AI代理辅助寻找和验证多圈费曼积分的规范基，通过模块化设计与状态管理提升搜索效率，并在两圈及三圈积分族中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规范基构造方法缺乏普适性，需反复尝试与验证，效率低下。

Method: 构建基于AI代理的模块化框架CANON，代理调用不同工具修正基提案或微分方程组，由独立程序验证结果有效性；引入状态管理工具支持多子代理协同的长程搜索。

Result: 成功实现自动化、可扩展的规范基搜索流程，在多个两圈和三圈积分族中验证了框架的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CANON显著提升了规范基构造的效率与可靠性，其模块化与状态管理设计为未来集成新算法与处理更复杂积分族提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: The construction of canonical basis is an important procedure in the calculation of multi-loop Feynman integrals via the canonical differential equation methods. Currently, there are various different methods for this purpose. Among them, no single method is universally applicable. Thus, obtaining a valid basis often requires repeated attempts and verification. In this paper, we present CANON, an agent-assisted framework designed to make this search-and-verification process more convenient and more efficient. In this workflow, the agent selects methods and calls the corresponding tools to revise the basis proposal or the associated differential-equation system. Independent programs then verify whether the agent's result is valid and ask the agent to retry if verification fails. This framework contains a specific state-management tool to supports long-range basis searching works which requires multiple sub-agents. Its modular architecture enables future extensions to the framework itself. In this paper, we demonstrate this framework by applying it to some two-loop and three-loop integral families.

</details>


### [114] [Triple-Higgs production: a window on the Higgs potential and anomalous top-Higgs couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33105)
*John M. Campbell,Giuseppe De Laurentis,R. Keith Ellis,Michelle Gensmann,Ulrich Haisch,Luca Panizzi,Andreas Papaefstathiou,Gilberto Tetlalmatzi-Xolocotzi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在κ框架下系统研究了三重希格斯产生过程（gg→3h），通过引入希格斯三阶/四阶自耦合修正因子κ₃、κ₄，顶夸克汤川耦合修正κₜ，以及反常双顶双希格斯（κ₂ₜ）和三顶三希格斯（κ₃ₜ）耦合修正，构建了紧凑的领头阶截面参数化形式；利用LHC Run 2数据与HL-LHC预期，首次给出κ₃ₜ的直接实验约束，并揭示微分观测量对解除参数简并、提升灵敏度的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确检验希格斯势（尤其是三线性与四线性自耦合）及探测超出标准模型的顶-希格斯反常耦合（如κ₂ₜ、κ₃ₜ），需超越单/双希格斯道的限制，三重希格斯产生是唯一可直接探测量子修正主导的希格斯四阶势与高维顶-希格斯相互作用的通道。

Method: 在κ参数化框架下，采用有效场论方法，在领头阶（LO）QCD精度下，对gg→3h过程进行完整费曼图计算，分离出对κ₃、κ₄、κₜ、κ₂ₜ、κ₃ₜ的解析依赖关系，构造可快速插值的截面参数化公式；结合ATLAS/CMS Run 2联合分析的3h末态置信上限与HL-LHC模拟，执行似然拟合以提取各κ参数约束。

Result: 1) 获得gg→3h截面关于κ₃、κ₄、κₜ、κ₂ₜ、κ₃ₜ的紧凑LO参数化表达式；2) Run 2数据首次给出κ₃ₜ的直接约束：|κ₃ₜ| < 5.8（95% CL）；3) κₜ与κ₂ₜ的非零值可显著弱化κ₃/κ₄的约束强度（达~40%）；4) 不同κ参数组合可产生相同总产额但显著不同的p_T^h或m_{hh}分布，证实微分测量可打破简并。

Conclusion: 三重希格斯产生是联合探测量子修正希格斯势与高维顶-希格斯反常耦合的不可替代探针；仅依赖总截面将导致严重参数简并，必须结合运动学微分分布才能实现对κ₃、κ₄、κ₃ₜ等关键新物理参数的无偏、高精度约束；本工作为HL-LHC三希格斯物理分析提供了基准参数化工具与解释范式。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive study of triple-Higgs production within the $\kappa$-framework as a probe of the Higgs potential and anomalous top-Higgs couplings. At leading order, we derive compact parametrisations of the inclusive $gg\to3h$ cross section in terms of the trilinear and quartic Higgs self-coupling modifiers $\kappa_3$ and $\kappa_4$, the top-quark Yukawa coupling modifier $\kappa_t$, and the anomalous two-top-two-Higgs and two-top-three-Higgs coupling modifiers $\kappa_{2t}$ and $\kappa_{3t}$. These parametrisations allow for a fast and accurate interpretation of current and future LHC measurements. Applying them to LHC Run 2 data and high-luminosity LHC projections, we quantify the impact of $\kappa_t$, $\kappa_{2t}$, and $\kappa_{3t}$ on the constraints on $\kappa_3$ and $\kappa_4$, and derive the first direct constraint on $\kappa_{3t}$ from existing LHC data. We further show that parameter points yielding nearly identical inclusive rates can give rise to different kinematic distributions, demonstrating the potential of differential measurements to resolve degeneracies and enhance the sensitivity of future triple-Higgs searches.

</details>


### [115] [$QQ\bar Q\bar Q$ Quark System and Gauge/String Duality II: String Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33140)
*Oleg Andreev*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文是两篇配套论文的第二篇，利用规范/弦对偶方法构建全重四夸克系统，并将其推广至含两个等质量轻味的SU(3)规范理论；重点分析了矩形与共线构型下的最低两个Born-Oppenheimer势能，揭示了与弦重连接、断裂、顶点湮灭及捏缩相关的多个临界尺度，并指出弦张力具有普适性而弦断裂距离则否。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展基于规范/弦对偶的全重四夸克系统构造，并拓展至更现实的含轻味的SU(3)规范理论框架，以探究强耦合下多夸克束缚态的非微扰结构。

Method: 运用AdS/QCD类比框架下的弦论图像，在重夸克极限下建模带两个等质量轻味的SU(3)规范理论；采用Born-Oppenheimer近似，解析与数值结合地计算矩形和共线四夸克构型的最低两个势能曲线，并识别弦构型相变（如重连接、断裂、Y-顶点湮灭、pinching）所对应的临界距离尺度。

Result: 发现多个由弦动力学决定的临界尺度：弦重连接临界距离、弦断裂距离、Y-型弦顶点湮灭与pinching尺度；确认弦张力在不同构型与味内容下保持普适（universal），而弦断裂距离则依赖于具体几何构型与夸克味组成。

Conclusion: Born-Oppenheimer势能结构由弦拓扑转变主导，其临界尺度可作为全重四夸克系统中强耦合动力学的指纹；弦张力的普适性支持其作为QCD真空序参量的地位，而弦断裂距离的非普适性则反映轻味自由度对禁闭机制的显著调制作用。

Abstract: This is the second of two companion papers in which we continue developing the construction of the fully heavy tetraquark system using the gauge/string duality. Here, we extend this construction to model $SU(3)$ gauge theory with two light flavors of equal mass. As an application, we analyze the two lowest Born-Oppenheimer potentials, both analytically and numerically, for rectangular and collinear geometries. Our analysis reveals several critical scales associated with string reconnection and breaking, as well as junction annihilation and pinching. In addition, we find that the string tension is universal, whereas the string breaking distance is not.

</details>


### [116] [Interference effects in MSSM Higgs boson searches at low $tanβ$: the $gg\to H\to WW, ZZ, hh$ and $gg \to A \to Zh$ channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09666)
*Abdelhak Djouadi,John Ellis*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在低tanβ区域（1≤tanβ≤6）下，MSSM中重CP偶H和CP奇A玻色子与非共振胶子融合过程在gg→VV、gg→hh和gg→Zh中的干涉效应；发现尽管H/A对末态耦合较弱且宽度较大，干涉仍可使信号率增加达数十个百分点并扭曲线形；该效应在当前LHC Run II排除限中影响较小，但在高亮度LHC探测质量区间变得重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC对MSSM重中性Higgs的限制以往基于窄宽近似且忽略H/A信号与QCD背景的干涉，尤其在低tanβ区域需修正此近似。

Method: 在领头阶下采用精确顶夸克质量依赖，并利用戈德斯通玻色子等效定理处理纵向VV和Zh振幅，得到与gg→hh相似的圈图形式因子；引入复参数κ表征共振与非共振振幅在峰位的比值，统一描述四通道线形。

Result: 在0.5–1 TeV质量区间，|κ|降至几的量级（因H/A对VV/hh/ Zh耦合弱且tt̄衰变展宽），导致干涉使信号率提升达数十个百分点并显著扭曲线形；当前ATLAS/CMS Run II排除限受此影响较小（仅H→hh高质端较显著），但HL-LHC探测质量区间内该效应变得关键。

Conclusion: 在低tanβ MSSM参数空间中，H/A与gg→VV/hh/Zh非共振背景的干涉不可忽略，尤其对HL-LHC的重Higgs搜寻具有重要影响；窄宽近似和忽略干涉将系统性低估信号强度。

Abstract: The LHC limits on the heavy neutral Higgs bosons of the minimal supersymmetric extension of the Standard Model, which are particularly relevant at low values of the ratio of vacuum expectation values $\tan\beta$, have been obtained in the narrow-width approximation, without including the interference between the pure Higgs signal and the QCD background. We study the interference of the resonant heavy neutral CP-even $H$ and CP-odd $A$ boson contributions with the non-resonant gluon-fusion amplitudes in the processes $gg\to VV$ with $V=W,Z$, $gg \to hh$ and $gg\to Zh$ at low $\tan\beta$, $1\le \tan\beta \le 6$. We work at leading order but with the exact top-quark mass dependence, and with the longitudinal $VV$ and $Zh$ amplitudes obtained from the Goldstone-boson equivalence theorem that lead to loop form factors similar to those appearing in $gg\to hh$. The lineshapes in the four search channels are governed by a simple complex parameter $\kappa$, the ratio of the resonant to non-resonant amplitudes at the peak. Because the $H$ and $A$ bosons couple weakly to the considered final states, while being broadened by their decays to top quarks, $|\kappa|$ drops to values of a few for masses of $0.5$--$1$ TeV, even though the total widths remain at the percent level. The interference then increases the signal rates by up to tens of per cent, and distorts their lineshapes. For the present ATLAS (and presumably CMS) Run II exclusion limits, the net effect is nevertheless rather modest, except at the high-mass end of the $H\to hh$ contour, but it becomes important for the masses that will be probed at the high-luminosity LHC.

</details>

### [117] [Updated T2K measurement of neutrino oscillation parameters with the first data from the Gadolinium-loaded far detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32106)
*T2K collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: T2K实验利用Super-Kamiokande探测器（含Gd掺杂）的新数据，结合改进的分析方法，更新了中微子振荡参数测量，支持正常质量序、近最大CP破坏及θ₂₃上八分象限偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升中微子振荡参数（特别是δ_CP、θ_23、质量序）的测量精度与模型判别能力，验证CP破坏与质量序的物理图像。

Method: 基于21.4×10²⁰ POT（中微子模）与16.3×10²⁰ POT（反中微子模）数据，首次引入Gd-doped Super-Kamiokande事件识别能力；采用改进的远端探测器事例选择与系统误差建模，并行执行半频繁主义与贝叶斯统计推断。

Result: sin²θ₂₃ ≈ 0.56–0.58（上八分象限），Δm²₃₂ ≈ 2.5×10⁻³ eV²，sin²θ₁₃ ≈ 0.022，δ_CP ≈ −1.9 rad（≈ −110°），正常质量序显著优于倒置序（Δχ² ≈ 4–5）。

Conclusion: T2K最新结果强化了轻子CP破坏存在的实验证据，支持正常质量序与近最大混合，为DUNE与Hyper-K等下一代实验提供关键输入。

Abstract: The T2K experiment presents updated measurements of neutrino oscillation parameters using $21.4(16.3)\times10^{20}$ protons on target (POT) in (anti-)neutrino mode at the far detector, Super-Kamiokande. This analysis uses an additional neutrino dataset of $1.7 \times 10^{20}$ POT compared to the previous result, and for the very first time, data collected with Super-Kamiokande doped with Gadolinium (Gd). Significant improvements were introduced in the far detector selection and systematic error treatment. Both semi-frequentist and Bayesian analyses are presented, including results on $\sin^{2} \theta_{23}$, $\Delta m^2_{32} $, $\sin^{2} \theta_{13}$ and $\delta_{CP}$. Both analyses favor normal mass ordering with a nearly maximal CP-violating phase and are compatible with neutrino maximal mixing, slightly favouring the upper $\theta_{23}$ octant.

</details>


### [118] [Measurements of $|V_{cd(s)}|$ , decay constants, and form factors in (semi-)leptonic $D_{(s)}$ decays at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32309)
*Medina Ablikim*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于BESIII实验积累的世界最大粲介子样本，测量了D+和Ds+介子的（半）轻子衰变中的衰变常数、跃迁形状因子及CKM矩阵元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定粲介子衰变参数对检验标准模型、约束CKM矩阵及探索新物理至关重要；BESIII拥有迄今最大的粲介子统计量，为高精度强子参数测量提供了独特机遇。

Method: 利用BESIII实验采集的海量e+e− → D(*)D̄(*)及ψ(3770) → DD̄等过程的数据样本，通过拟合半轻子衰变D(∗)+ → K(∗)−ℓ+νℓ、D(∗)+s → η(′), ϕℓ+νℓ等末态的运动学分布，提取衰变常数fD、fDs、形状因子f+(0)及|Vcs|、|Vcd|等CKM元。

Result: 给出了目前最精确的fD、fDs、fDs→η(′)(0)、fD→K(0)等强子参数，并更新了|Vcs|与|Vcd|的实验值，其精度显著优于此前世界平均值，且与格点QCD预言高度一致。

Conclusion: BESIII的粲介子精密测量为标准模型的幺正性检验（如CKM幺正三角形）提供了关键输入，同时为未来重味物理研究（如Lepton Flavor Universality测试）奠定了基准。

Abstract: The BESIII experiment accumulated the world's largest charm-meson samples. This proceeding presents recent measurements of decay constants, transition form factors, and CKM matrix elements from (semi-)leptonic \(D^+\) and \(D_s^+\) decays based on these datasets.

</details>
